cgroup\+\_\+add\+\_\+cftypes -\/ add an array of cftypes to a subsystem @ss\+: target cgroup subsystem @cfts\+: zero-\/length name terminated array of cftypes

Register @cfts to @ss. Files described by @cfts are created for all existing cgroups to which @ss is attached and all future cgroups will have them too. This function can be called anytime whether @ss is attached or not.

Returns 0 on successful registration, -\/errno on failure. Note that this function currently returns 0 as long as @cfts registration is successful even if some file creation attempts on existing cgroups fail. Here is the call graph for this function\+:

cgroup\+\_\+apply\+\_\+control -\/ apply control mask updates to the subtree @cgrp\+: root of the target subtree

subsystems can be enabled and disabled in a subtree using the following steps.


\begin{DoxyEnumerate}
\item Call \mbox{\hyperlink{cgroup_2cgroup_8c_aad3561142cafdf3275cb1b45d66d76e2}{cgroup\+\_\+save\+\_\+control()}} to stash the current state.
\item Update -\/$>$subtree\+\_\+control masks in the subtree as desired.
\item Call \mbox{\hyperlink{cgroup_2cgroup_8c_a19691d198ec77937b2f7d1514e9b008a}{cgroup\+\_\+apply\+\_\+control()}} to apply the changes.
\item Optionally perform other related operations.
\item Call \mbox{\hyperlink{cgroup_2cgroup_8c_a1305f3486e4bc06d2e5d636e9d6a77fe}{cgroup\+\_\+finalize\+\_\+control()}} to finish up.
\end{DoxyEnumerate}

This function implements step 3 and propagates the mask changes throughout @cgrp\textquotesingle{}s subtree, updates csses accordingly and perform process migrations. Here is the call graph for this function\+:

cgroup\+\_\+apply\+\_\+control\+\_\+disable -\/ kill or hide csses according to control @cgrp\+: root of the target subtree

Walk @cgrp\textquotesingle{}s subtree and kill and hide csses so that they match \mbox{\hyperlink{cgroup_2cgroup_8c_a285bd4beda77357f88f5d04f6e1e2736}{cgroup\+\_\+ss\+\_\+mask()}} and cgroup\+\_\+visible\+\_\+mask().

A css is hidden when the userland requests it to be disabled while other subsystems are still depending on it. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} css must not actively control resources and be in the vanilla state if it\textquotesingle{}s made visible again later. Controllers which may be depended upon should provide -\/$>$css\+\_\+reset() for this purpose. Here is the call graph for this function\+:

cgroup\+\_\+apply\+\_\+control\+\_\+enable -\/ enable or show csses according to control @cgrp\+: root of the target subtree

Walk @cgrp\textquotesingle{}s subtree and create new csses or make the existing ones visible. A css is created invisible if it\textquotesingle{}s being implicitly enabled through dependency. An invisible css is made visible when the userland explicitly enables it.

Returns 0 on success, -\/errno on failure. On failure, csses which have been processed already aren\textquotesingle{}t cleaned up. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} caller is responsible for cleaning up with \mbox{\hyperlink{cgroup_2cgroup_8c_a92f0f057e48c2427acbab8995b6d20f0}{cgroup\+\_\+apply\+\_\+control\+\_\+disable()}}. cgroup\+\_\+can\+\_\+fork -\/ called on a new task before the process is exposed @child\+: the child process

This prepares a new css\+\_\+set for the child process which the child will be attached to in \mbox{\hyperlink{cgroup_2cgroup_8c_ace93e156c02ebfd2bb8481618606b70f}{cgroup\+\_\+post\+\_\+fork()}}. This calls the subsystem can\+\_\+fork() callbacks. If the \mbox{\hyperlink{cgroup_2cgroup_8c_abe62e05b6941fcbd0d6c376c19a01b7b}{cgroup\+\_\+can\+\_\+fork()}} callback returns an error, the fork aborts with that error code. This allows for a cgroup subsystem to conditionally allow or deny new forks. Here is the call graph for this function\+:

cgroup\+\_\+css\+\_\+set\+\_\+fork -\/ find or create a css\+\_\+set for a child process @kargs\+: the arguments passed to create the child process

This functions finds or creates a new css\+\_\+set which the child process will be attached to in \mbox{\hyperlink{cgroup_2cgroup_8c_ace93e156c02ebfd2bb8481618606b70f}{cgroup\+\_\+post\+\_\+fork()}}. By default, the child process will be given the same css\+\_\+set as its parent.

If C\+L\+O\+N\+E\+\_\+\+I\+N\+T\+O\+\_\+\+C\+G\+R\+O\+UP is specified this function will try to find an existing css\+\_\+set which includes the requested cgroup and if not create a new css\+\_\+set that the child will be attached to later. If this function succeeds it will hold cgroup\+\_\+threadgroup\+\_\+rwsem on return. If C\+L\+O\+N\+E\+\_\+\+I\+N\+T\+O\+\_\+\+C\+G\+R\+O\+UP is requested this function will grab cgroup mutex before grabbing cgroup\+\_\+threadgroup\+\_\+rwsem and will hold a reference to the target cgroup. Here is the call graph for this function\+:
% FIG 50

{\footnotesize\ttfamily static \mbox{\hyperlink{dcache_8h_ad65b480f8c8270356b45a9890f6499ae}{int}} cgroup\+\_\+destroy\+\_\+locked (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{uapi_2linux_2bpf_8h_acce2557c3a83239db911bfaccf1e909b}{cgroup}} $\ast$}]{cgrp }\end{DoxyParamCaption}) \&\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [static]}}

cgroup\+\_\+destroy\+\_\+locked -\/ the first stage of cgroup destruction @cgrp\+: cgroup to be destroyed

css\textquotesingle{}s make use of percpu refcnts whose killing latency shouldn\textquotesingle{}t be exposed to userland and are R\+CU protected. Also, cgroup core needs to guarantee that css\+\_\+tryget\+\_\+online() won\textquotesingle{}t succeed by the time -\/$>$css\+\_\+offline() is invoked. To satisfy all the requirements, destruction is implemented in the following two steps.

s1. Verify @cgrp can be destroyed and mark it dying. Remove all userland visible parts and start killing the percpu refcnts of css\textquotesingle{}s. Set up so that the next stage will be kicked off once all the percpu refcnts are confirmed to be killed.

s2. Invoke -\/$>$css\+\_\+offline(), mark the cgroup dead and proceed with the rest of destruction. Once all cgroup references are gone, the cgroup is R\+C\+U-\/freed.

This function implements s1. After this step, @cgrp is gone as far as the userland is concerned and a new cgroup with the same name may be created. As cgroup doesn\textquotesingle{}t care about the names internally, this doesn\textquotesingle{}t cause any problem. Here is the call graph for this function\+:
% FIG 55
\mbox{\Hypertarget{cgroup_2cgroup_8c_ac8694a01fd73d2fd636abde8a103d09d}\label{cgroup_2cgroup_8c_ac8694a01fd73d2fd636abde8a103d09d}}

cgroup\+\_\+e\+\_\+css -\/ obtain a cgroup\textquotesingle{}s effective css for the specified subsystem @cgrp\+: the cgroup of interest @ss\+: the subsystem of interest

Find and get the effective css of @cgrp for @ss. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} effective css is defined as the matching css of the nearest ancestor including self which has @ss enabled. If @ss is not mounted on the hierarchy @cgrp is on, the root css is returned, so this function always returns a valid css.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} returned css is not guaranteed to be online, and therefore it is the callers responsibility to try get a reference for it. Here is the call graph for this function\+:
% FIG 61

cgroup\+\_\+get\+\_\+e\+\_\+css -\/ get a cgroup\textquotesingle{}s effective css for the specified subsystem @cgrp\+: the cgroup of interest @ss\+: the subsystem of interest

Find and get the effective css of @cgrp for @ss. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} effective css is defined as the matching css of the nearest ancestor including self which has @ss enabled. If @ss is not mounted on the hierarchy @cgrp is on, the root css is returned, so this function always returns a valid css. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} returned css must be put using \mbox{\hyperlink{linux_2cgroup_8h_ac89132b6151b637f26988df6e131f5e5}{css\+\_\+put()}}. cgroup\+\_\+get\+\_\+from\+\_\+path -\/ lookup and get a cgroup from its default hierarchy path @path\+: path on the default hierarchy

Find the cgroup at @path on the default hierarchy, increment its reference count and return it. Returns pointer to the found cgroup on success, E\+R\+R\+\_\+\+P\+TR(-\/E\+N\+O\+E\+NT) if @path doesn\textquotesingle{}t exist and E\+R\+R\+\_\+\+P\+TR(-\/E\+N\+O\+T\+D\+IR) if @path points to a non-\/directory. {\footnotesize\ttfamily struct \mbox{\hyperlink{uapi_2linux_2bpf_8h_acce2557c3a83239db911bfaccf1e909b}{cgroup}}$\ast$ cgroup\+\_\+kn\+\_\+lock\+\_\+live (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structkernfs__node}{kernfs\+\_\+node}} $\ast$}]{kn,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{include_2linux_2types_8h_afaa87723b8417d40fcf45b7330261ef9}{bool}}}]{drain\+\_\+offline }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

cgroup\+\_\+kn\+\_\+lock\+\_\+live -\/ locking helper for cgroup kernfs methods @kn\+: the \mbox{\hyperlink{structkernfs__node}{kernfs\+\_\+node}} being serviced @drain\+\_\+offline\+: perform offline draining on the cgroup

This helper is to be used by a cgroup kernfs method currently servicing @kn. It breaks the active protection, performs cgroup locking and verifies that the associated cgroup is alive. Returns the cgroup if alive; otherwise, N\+U\+LL. A successful return should be undone by a matching \mbox{\hyperlink{cgroup_2cgroup_8c_a5808e21adbb735b336c1ac679347c4f4}{cgroup\+\_\+kn\+\_\+unlock()}} invocation. If @drain\+\_\+offline is true, the cgroup is drained of offlining csses before return.

Any cgroup kernfs method implementation which requires locking the associated cgroup should use this helper. It avoids nesting cgroup locking under kernfs active protection and allows all kernfs operations including self-\/removal. Here is the call graph for this function\+:

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{dcache_8h_ad65b480f8c8270356b45a9890f6499ae}{int}} cgroup\+\_\+migrate (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct task\+\_\+struct $\ast$}]{leader,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{include_2linux_2types_8h_afaa87723b8417d40fcf45b7330261ef9}{bool}}}]{threadgroup,  }\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structcgroup__mgctx}{cgroup\+\_\+mgctx}} $\ast$}]{mgctx }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

cgroup\+\_\+migrate -\/ migrate a process or task to a cgroup @leader\+: the leader of the process or the task to migrate @threadgroup\+: whether @leader points to the whole process or a single task @mgctx\+: migration context

Migrate a process or task denoted by @leader. If migrating a process, the caller must be holding cgroup\+\_\+threadgroup\+\_\+rwsem. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} caller is also responsible for invoking \mbox{\hyperlink{cgroup_2cgroup_8c_ae1c005f2422d148d0359a2032fb9e291}{cgroup\+\_\+migrate\+\_\+add\+\_\+src()}} and \mbox{\hyperlink{cgroup_2cgroup_8c_a10644a3466731282fcec4423bbad0343}{cgroup\+\_\+migrate\+\_\+prepare\+\_\+dst()}} on the targets before invoking this function and following up with \mbox{\hyperlink{cgroup_2cgroup_8c_aa7e924fc6912f50590ab05ee0f7bb029}{cgroup\+\_\+migrate\+\_\+finish()}}.

As long as a controller\textquotesingle{}s -\/$>$can\+\_\+attach() doesn\textquotesingle{}t fail, this function is guaranteed to succeed. This means that, excluding -\/$>$can\+\_\+attach() failure, when migrating multiple targets, the success or failure can be decided for all targets by invoking group\+\_\+migrate\+\_\+prepare\+\_\+dst() before actually starting migrating. \doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{cgroup\_migrate\_add\_src()}{cgroup\_migrate\_add\_src()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily void cgroup\+\_\+migrate\+\_\+add\+\_\+src (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct css\+\_\+set $\ast$}]{src\+\_\+cset,  }\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{uapi_2linux_2bpf_8h_acce2557c3a83239db911bfaccf1e909b}{cgroup}} $\ast$}]{dst\+\_\+cgrp,  }\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structcgroup__mgctx}{cgroup\+\_\+mgctx}} $\ast$}]{mgctx }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

cgroup\+\_\+migrate\+\_\+add\+\_\+src -\/ add a migration source css\+\_\+set @src\+\_\+cset\+: the source css\+\_\+set to add @dst\+\_\+cgrp\+: the destination cgroup @mgctx\+: migration context

Tasks belonging to @src\+\_\+cset are about to be migrated to @dst\+\_\+cgrp. Pin @src\+\_\+cset and add it to @mgctx-\/$>$src\+\_\+csets, which should later be cleaned up by \mbox{\hyperlink{cgroup_2cgroup_8c_aa7e924fc6912f50590ab05ee0f7bb029}{cgroup\+\_\+migrate\+\_\+finish()}}.

This function may be called without holding cgroup\+\_\+threadgroup\+\_\+rwsem even if the target is a process. Threads may be created and destroyed but as long as cgroup\+\_\+mutex is not dropped, no new css\+\_\+set can be put into play and the preloaded css\+\_\+sets are guaranteed to cover all migrations. cgroup\+\_\+migrate\+\_\+add\+\_\+task -\/ add a migration target task to a migration context @task\+: target task @mgctx\+: target migration context

Add @task, which is a migration target, to @mgctx-\/$>$tset. This function becomes noop if @task doesn\textquotesingle{}t need to be migrated. @task\textquotesingle{}s css\+\_\+set should have been added as a migration source and @task-\/$>$cg\+\_\+list will be moved from the css\+\_\+set\textquotesingle{}s tasks list to mg\+\_\+tasks one. cgroup\+\_\+migrate\+\_\+execute -\/ migrate a taskset @mgctx\+: migration context

Migrate tasks in @mgctx as setup by migration preparation functions. This function fails iff one of the -\/$>$can\+\_\+attach callbacks fails and guarantees that either all or none of the tasks in @mgctx are migrated. @mgctx is consumed regardless of success. cgroup\+\_\+migrate\+\_\+prepare\+\_\+dst -\/ prepare destination css\+\_\+sets for migration @mgctx\+: migration context

Tasks are about to be moved and all the source css\+\_\+sets have been preloaded to @mgctx-\/$>$preloaded\+\_\+src\+\_\+csets. This function looks up and pins all destination css\+\_\+sets, links each to its source, and append them to @mgctx-\/$>$preloaded\+\_\+dst\+\_\+csets.

This function must be called after \mbox{\hyperlink{cgroup_2cgroup_8c_ae1c005f2422d148d0359a2032fb9e291}{cgroup\+\_\+migrate\+\_\+add\+\_\+src()}} has been called on each migration source css\+\_\+set. After migration is performed using \mbox{\hyperlink{cgroup_2cgroup_8c_a91d7710c20f468bb8371512b15737bc2}{cgroup\+\_\+migrate()}}, \mbox{\hyperlink{cgroup_2cgroup_8c_aa7e924fc6912f50590ab05ee0f7bb029}{cgroup\+\_\+migrate\+\_\+finish()}} must be called on @mgctx. cgroup\+\_\+on\+\_\+dfl -\/ test whether a cgroup is on the default hierarchy @cgrp\+: the cgroup of interest

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} default hierarchy is the v2 interface of cgroup and this function can be used to test whether a cgroup is on the default hierarchy for cases where a subsystem should behave differently depending on the interface version.

List of changed behaviors\+:


\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Mount options \char`\"{}noprefix\char`\"{}, \char`\"{}xattr\char`\"{}, \char`\"{}clone\+\_\+children\char`\"{}, \char`\"{}release\+\_\+agent\char`\"{} and \char`\"{}name\char`\"{} are disallowed.
\item When mounting an existing superblock, mount options should match.
\item Remount is disallowed.
\item rename(2) is disallowed.
\item \char`\"{}tasks\char`\"{} is removed. Everything should be at process granularity. Use \char`\"{}cgroup.\+procs\char`\"{} instead.
\item \char`\"{}cgroup.\+procs\char`\"{} is not sorted. pids will be unique unless they got recycled in-\/between reads.
\item \char`\"{}release\+\_\+agent\char`\"{} and \char`\"{}notify\+\_\+on\+\_\+release\char`\"{} are removed. Replacement notification mechanism will be implemented.
\item \char`\"{}cgroup.\+clone\+\_\+children\char`\"{} is removed.
\item \char`\"{}cgroup.\+subtree\+\_\+populated\char`\"{} is available. Its value is 0 if the cgroup and its descendants contain no task; otherwise, 1. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} file also generates kernfs notification which can be monitored through poll and \mbox{[}di\mbox{]}notify when the value of the file changes.
\item cpuset\+: tasks will be kept in empty cpusets when hotplug happens and take masks of ancestors with non-\/empty cpus/mems, instead of being moved to an ancestor.
\item cpuset\+: a task can be moved into an empty cpuset, and again it takes masks of ancestors.
\item blkcg\+: blk-\/throttle becomes properly hierarchical.
\item debug\+: disallowed on the default hierarchy. 
\end{DoxyItemize}Here is the caller graph for this function\+:
% FIG 160
\mbox{\Hypertarget{cgroup_2cgroup_8c_aef7f4e3bba6ef25ff79ed234031c7456}\label{cgroup_2cgroup_8c_aef7f4e3bba6ef25ff79ed234031c7456}}

cgroup\+\_\+parse\+\_\+float -\/ parse a floating number @input\+: input string @dec\+\_\+shift\+: number of decimal digits to shift @v\+: output

Parse a decimal floating point number in @input and store the result in @v with decimal point right shifted @dec\+\_\+shift times. For example, if @input is \char`\"{}12.\+3456\char`\"{} and @dec\+\_\+shift is 3, $\ast$@v will be set to 12345. Returns 0 on success, -\/errno otherwise.

cgroup\+\_\+rm\+\_\+cftypes -\/ remove an array of cftypes from a subsystem @cfts\+: zero-\/length name terminated array of cftypes

Unregister @cfts. Files described by @cfts are removed from all existing cgroups and all future cgroups won\textquotesingle{}t have them either. This function can be called anytime whether @cfts\textquotesingle{} subsys is attached or not.

Returns 0 on successful unregistration, -\/E\+N\+O\+E\+NT if @cfts is not registered. Here is the call graph for this function\+:

cgroup\+\_\+update\+\_\+dfl\+\_\+csses -\/ update css assoc of a subtree in default hierarchy @cgrp\+: root of the subtree to update csses for

@cgrp\textquotesingle{}s control masks have changed and its subtree\textquotesingle{}s css associations need to be updated accordingly. This function looks up all css\+\_\+sets which are attached to the subtree, creates the matching updated css\+\_\+sets and migrates the tasks to the new ones. {\footnotesize\ttfamily static void cgroup\+\_\+update\+\_\+populated (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{uapi_2linux_2bpf_8h_acce2557c3a83239db911bfaccf1e909b}{cgroup}} $\ast$}]{cgrp,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{include_2linux_2types_8h_afaa87723b8417d40fcf45b7330261ef9}{bool}}}]{populated }\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [static]}}

cgroup\+\_\+update\+\_\+populated -\/ update the populated count of a cgroup @cgrp\+: the target cgroup @populated\+: inc or dec populated count

One of the css\+\_\+sets associated with @cgrp is either getting its first task or losing the last. Update @cgrp-\/$>$nr\+\_\+populated\+\_\+$\ast$ accordingly. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} count is propagated towards root so that a given cgroup\textquotesingle{}s nr\+\_\+populated\+\_\+children is zero iff none of its descendants contain any tasks.

@cgrp\textquotesingle{}s interface file \char`\"{}cgroup.\+populated\char`\"{} is zero if both @cgrp-\/$>$nr\+\_\+populated\+\_\+csets and @cgrp-\/$>$nr\+\_\+populated\+\_\+children are zero and 1 otherwise. When the sum changes from or to zero, userland is notified that the content of the interface file has changed. This can be used to detect when @cgrp and its descendants become populated or empty. {\footnotesize\ttfamily struct cgroup\+\_\+subsys\+\_\+state$\ast$ css\+\_\+next\+\_\+child (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct cgroup\+\_\+subsys\+\_\+state $\ast$}]{pos,  }\item[{struct cgroup\+\_\+subsys\+\_\+state $\ast$}]{parent }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

css\+\_\+next\+\_\+child -\/ find the next child of a given css @pos\+: the current position (N\+U\+LL to initiate traversal) @parent\+: css whose children to walk

This function returns the next child of @parent and should be called under either cgroup\+\_\+mutex or R\+CU read lock. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} only requirement is that @parent and @pos are accessible. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} next sibling is guaranteed to be returned regardless of their states.

If a subsystem synchronizes -\/$>$css\+\_\+online() and the start of iteration, a css which finished -\/$>$css\+\_\+online() is guaranteed to be visible in the future iterations and will stay visible until the last reference is put. A css which hasn\textquotesingle{}t finished -\/$>$css\+\_\+online() or already finished -\/$>$css\+\_\+offline() may show up during traversal. It\textquotesingle{}s each subsystem\textquotesingle{}s responsibility to synchronize against on/offlining. Here is the call graph for this function\+:

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{css\_next\_descendant\_post()}{css\_next\_descendant\_post()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily struct cgroup\+\_\+subsys\+\_\+state$\ast$ css\+\_\+next\+\_\+descendant\+\_\+post (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct cgroup\+\_\+subsys\+\_\+state $\ast$}]{pos,  }\item[{struct cgroup\+\_\+subsys\+\_\+state $\ast$}]{root }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

css\+\_\+next\+\_\+descendant\+\_\+post -\/ find the next descendant for post-\/order walk @pos\+: the current position (N\+U\+LL to initiate traversal) @root\+: css whose descendants to walk

To be used by css\+\_\+for\+\_\+each\+\_\+descendant\+\_\+post(). Find the next descendant to visit for post-\/order traversal of @root\textquotesingle{}s descendants. @root is included in the iteration and the last node to be visited.

While this function requires cgroup\+\_\+mutex or R\+CU read locking, it doesn\textquotesingle{}t require the whole traversal to be contained in a single critical section. This function will return the correct next descendant as long as both @pos and @cgroup are accessible and @pos is a descendant of @cgroup.

If a subsystem synchronizes -\/$>$css\+\_\+online() and the start of iteration, a css which finished -\/$>$css\+\_\+online() is guaranteed to be visible in the future iterations and will stay visible until the last reference is put. A css which hasn\textquotesingle{}t finished -\/$>$css\+\_\+online() or already finished -\/$>$css\+\_\+offline() may show up during traversal. It\textquotesingle{}s each subsystem\textquotesingle{}s responsibility to synchronize against on/offlining. Here is the call graph for this function\+:

kill\+\_\+css -\/ destroy a css @css\+: css to destroy

This function initiates destruction of @css by removing cgroup interface files and putting its base reference. -\/$>$css\+\_\+offline() will be invoked asynchronously once css\+\_\+tryget\+\_\+online() is guaranteed to fail and when the reference count reaches zero, @css will be released. task\+\_\+cgroup\+\_\+path -\/ cgroup path of a task in the first cgroup hierarchy @task\+: target task @buf\+: the buffer to write the path into @buflen\+: the length of the buffer

Determine @task\textquotesingle{}s cgroup on the first (the one with the lowest non-\/zero hierarchy\+\_\+id) cgroup hierarchy and copy its path into @buf. This function grabs cgroup\+\_\+mutex and shouldn\textquotesingle{}t be used inside locks used by cgroup controller callbacks.
